def gradient_optimize(h, n, alphas, iters, deadline, step_init=0.5, momentum=True, max_exact_evals maybe):
    h = project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = compute_c5(h,n)
    last_smooth = None
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       cur = h.copy()
       y = cur.copy()
       h_prev = cur.copy()
       step = step_init
       stuck = 0
       for it in range(iters[alpha]):
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          f_y, g = smooth_c5_grad(y, n, alpha)
          # if gradient norm tiny break
          h_new, f_new, step = proj_line_search(y, g, f_y, n, alpha, step)
          if f_new <= f_y - 1e-13:
             stuck=0
             h_prev, cur = cur, h_new
             if momentum:
                mom = it / (it + 3.0)
                y = project_box_sum(cur + mom*(cur - h_prev), n/2.0)
             else:
                y = cur.copy()
             if time.time() %? evaluate exact every 10 or when f decreased: 
                c_ex=compute_c5(cur,n); update best
          else:
             stuck +=1
             step *= 0.5
             if step < 1e-6: break
       # after alpha, set h = best of cur and best_h? Use cur if smooth lower; but exact best maybe not. For next alpha, start from cur (maybe lower smooth) but if exact worsened, could still explore. Keep h = cur if no exception; also if best_h exact better? Starting from best exact may be safer. We can set h = best_h.copy() if best exact much lower? Hard.
       h = cur.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```

def gradient_optimize(...):
  cur=project
  best=cur
  for alpha in alphas:

    for it in range(iters):
      f_y,g=...
      h_new,f_new,step=line_search...
      if f_new < f_y - 1e-12:
          old=cur; cur=h_new; stale=0
          if it%10==0: update best exact
          # optional momentum: y=project(cur + 0.5*(cur-old))
      else:
          stale+=1; step=max(step*0.3,1e-6)
          if stale>30:
             # nudge cur with small random noise and reproject; break alpha
             cur=project(cur + rng.normal(0,0.01,n),target)
             y=cur.copy()
             stale=0; step=step_init
             if it>20: break?

Need line search uses smooth_objective each trial. If f_new lower but not Armijo, accept? We track best and if best improves, accept? Yes. In line_search return best_h if best_f < f_y - 1e-12, even if Armijo failed, because descent. This may violate Lipschitz but okay.

Implement:
```
def proj_line_search(y, grad, f_y, n, alpha, step):
    target=n/2
    g2=float(np.dot(grad,grad))
    if g2 < 1e-24: return y.copy(), f_y, step*0.5
    best_h=y.copy(); best_f=f_y; trial_step=step
    for _ in range(14):
       h1=project_box_sum(y - trial_step*grad, target)
       f1=smooth_objective(h1,n,alpha)
       if f1 < best_f:
           best_h, best_f = h1, f1
       if f1 <= f_y - 1e-5*trial_step*g2/objective? Use 1e-4.
       if ...: return h1, f1, min(trial_step*2, 2.0)
       trial_step *= 0.5
    if best_f < f_y - 1e-12:
       return best_h, best_f, trial_step
    return y.copy(), f_y, trial_step*0.5
```
Armijo criterion: f <= f_y - 1e-4 * step * g2. For convex with L, should. If not but descent, in loop we might not return; but after loop accept best. Good.

Potential issue: `smooth_objective` uses alpha; if f not finite? handle.

Now `coord_descent` improved. Let's write efficient version.

```
def coord_descent(h,n,max_passes,deadline,gold=22,active_tol=0.2):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    target=n/2
    for pass in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
       cmax=float(c.max())
       improved=False
       # maybe shuffle? deterministic order.
       for p in range(n):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          h0=h[p]
          if h0 <=1e-12 or h0 >=1-1e-12: continue
          m_lo=max(0,n-1-p); m_hi=min(2*n-2,n-1+p)
          if m_hi < m_lo: continue
          # affected length = m_hi-m_lo+1
          # If max affected value < cmax - something and unaffected cmax high? Actually if no affected close to cmax, p cannot reduce cmax; but if p in active lag, affected includes cmax.
          # quick skip: if np.max(c[m_lo:m_hi+1]) < cmax - 1.0: maybe no. But coordinate may raise unaffected? We only lower if reduce max; if p not in any max, skip. If affected max < cmax - 0.1, changing p cannot lower cmax. However it may lower second max below cmax? cmax remains unless also changes active? no. So skip safe if no affected active. Use active_tol=0.25. But if all active lags involve p? affected max would cmax.
          aff_c=c[m_lo:m_hi+1]
          if aff_c.max() < cmax - 0.5: continue  # maybe
          ms=np.arange(m_lo,m_hi+1)
          jf=p + ms - (n-1); jb=p - ms + (n-1)

Let's write `smooth_c5_grad` efficient:
```
def smooth_c5_grad(h,n,alpha):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = alpha*(c-m)
    # z max 0, but if numerical positive small, subtract max
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    if zmax != 0.0: z -= zmax
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    f = (m + (zmax + np.log(s))/alpha) * (2.0/n)
    w /= s
    if n==1: return f, np.zeros(1)
    pos = get_pos(n)
    center = n-1
    wA=np.zeros(n); wB=np.zeros(n)
    wA[0]=w[center]
    if pos:
       wA[1:] = w[center+1:center+n]
       wB[1:] = w[center-1:center-n-1:-1]
    else:
       wA[1:] = w[center-1:center-n-1:-1]
       wB[1:] = w[center+1:center+n]
    w0=w[center]
    prefA=np.zeros(n); prefB=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1:
       prefA[1:] = np.cumsum(wA[1:])
       prefB[1:] = np.cumsum(wB[1:])
    idx=np.arange(n)
    const = prefA[n-1-idx] + prefB[idx]
    wt = wA[1:] + wB[1:]
    R = np.zeros(2*n-1)
    if n>1:
       R[center+1:] = wt
       R[:center] = wt[::-1]
    y = np.convolve(h, R, mode='full')[center:center+n]
    dx=2.0/n
    return f, dx*(w0*(1.0-2.0*h) + const - y)
```
Check slices when pos False: `w[center-1:center-n-1:-1]`: e.g. n=5 center=4, start3 stop 4-5-1=-2 step -1 -> indices 3,2,1,0 length4 (L=1..4) good. `w[center+1:center+n]` start5 stop9 length4 (5,6,7,8). Good.
For n=2: center1; pos true wA[1:]=w[2:3] index2; wB[1:]=w[0:-2:-1]? center-1=0 stop center-n-1=1-2-1=-2 step -1 -> index0 length1. good.
For pos false wA[1:]=w[0:-2:-1] index0; wB[1:]=w[2:3]. good.

`np.convolve(h,R,'full')` when R all zeros (n=1) but handled.

Can reuse `idx_arange` per n cached to avoid creating? Use `np.arange(n)` each gradient, small. Could cache in dict? Top-level `_ARANGE_CACHE={}`. But no need.

`proj_line_search` implementation:
```
def proj_line_search(y, grad, f_y, n, alpha, step):
    target=n*0.5
    g2=float(np.dot(grad,grad))
    if not np.isfinite(g2) or g2<1e-20: return y.copy(), f_y, max(step*0.5,1e-4)
    best_h=y.copy(); best_f=f_y; s=step
    # We may try up to 16 scales: s, 0.5s,... and maybe 2s? No.
    for _ in range(16):
       h1=project_box_sum(y - s*grad, target)
       f1=smooth_objective(h1,n,alpha)
       if f1 < best_f: best_h, best_f = h1, f1
       if f1 <= f_y - 1e-4*s*g2:
           return h1, f1, min(s*1.5, 0.5)
       s *= 0.5
       if s < 1e-7: break
    if best_f < f_y - 1e-14:
       return best_h, best_f, max(s,1e-4)
    return y.copy(), f_y, max(s*0.5,1e-5)
```
If no descent but smaller step maybe? We loop to 1e-7. Good.

def gradient_optimize(h,n,schedule,deadline,step_init=0.1,final_cd=False):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    cur=h.copy()
    for alpha,iters in schedule:
       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       y=cur.copy(); step=step_init; stale=0
       for it in range(iters):
          if time.time()>=deadline: break
          f_y,g=smooth_c5_grad(y,n,alpha)
          h_new,f_new,step=proj_line_search(y,g,f_y,n,alpha,step)
          if f_new < f_y - 1e-13:
             # simple momentum
             y = project_box_sum(cur + 0.3*(h_new - y), target) ?
```

   if cex<best_c: best...
# momentum optional: y = project(cur + 0.5*(cur-old)) if it>0 else cur
mom = 0.5*min(1.0, it/(it+3.0))

# but if this y has worse smooth, maybe line search still okay.
```

if stale>40:
   # maybe break alpha; set y=cur; step=step_init
   if stale>80: break
```

`compute_c5(h,n)`:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')))*dx
```

`project_box_sum(y,target)`:
```
def project_box_sum(y, target):
    y=np.asarray(y,dtype=float)
    if y.size==0: return y
    fmin=float(np.min(y)); fmax=float(np.max(y))
    # If y already close feasible
    if fmin >= -1e-12 and fmax <= 1+1e-12:
       s=float(np.sum(y))
       if abs(s-target) <= 1e-9:
          h=np.clip(y,0.0,1.0); diff=target-h.sum(); ...return
    lo=-fmax
    hi=1.0-fmin
    # if hi<lo? fmax could >? lo negative, hi maybe <lo if y all >1? Example ymin=2, fmax=3: lo=-3, hi=-1; target in [0,n], S(lo)=? t=-3 => all <=0, sum0; hi=-1 => all >=1? y+(-1) min1, sum n. good hi>lo.
    for _ in range(40):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=float(np.sum(np.clip(y+mid,0.0,1.0)))
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(y+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-float(np.sum(h))
    if diff != 0.0:
       mask=(h>1e-12) & (h<1.0-1e-12)
       if mask.any():
          h=h.copy(); h[mask]+=diff/float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
       else:
          # adjust all by small? But can't if at bounds. Use distribute to least bound
          h=h.copy()
          if diff > 0:
             idx=np.argmin(h)
             # add to all zeros? target maybe n/2, if all 1? impossible; choose many
             idxs=np.where(h < h.max())[0]
             h[idxs]+=diff/float(len(idxs))
          else:
             idxs=np.where(h > h.min())[0]
             h[idxs]+=diff/float(len(idxs))
       h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return h
```
The fallback might not make exact if diff large, but diff tiny. Use `np.where` to choose coordinates not at bound. Simpler `mask = (h > 0) & (h < 1)`; if none and target strictly between, all at bounds but maybe some at 0/1; choose `if diff>0: idx=np.where(h<1)[0] else idx=np.where(h>0)[0]`; len idx >0 if target not 0/n. Add diff/len, clip. Good.

Potential issue: bisection S(lo) may equal 0? lo=-fmax ensures max(y+lo)=0, so all ≤0; sum 0. hi=1-fmin ensures min≥1, sum n. Good. If target=0/n (not our target), works.

`detect_pos(n)`:
```
POS_CACHE={}
def detect_pos(n):
  if n in POS_CACHE: return POS_CACHE[n]
  if n<2: POS_CACHE[n]=True; return True
  h_ref=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
  if n>1:
    A1=float(np.dot(h_ref[:-1], 1.0-h_ref[1:]))
    B1=float(np.dot(h_ref[1:], 1.0-h_ref[:-1]))
    c=np.correlate(h_ref,1.0-h_ref,mode='full')
    center=n-1
    val_plus=c[center+1]
    val_minus=c[center-1]
    pos = abs(val_plus-A1) <= abs(val_minus-A1)
  else: pos=True
  POS_CACHE[n]=pos
  return pos
```
If h_ref endpoints 0/1, sum not target but okay. A1/B1 with h_ref=0 and1: difference good. If n=2, center+1 index2 exists? length3 indices0,1,2. center=1. yes.

`smooth_objective`:
```
def smooth_objective(h,n,alpha):
  c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
  m=float(np.max(c))
  if alpha <= 1.0:
     return m*(2.0/n)
  z=alpha*(c-m)
  zmax=float(np.max(z))
  z=z-zmax
  s=float(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
  if s <= 0: s=1.0
  return (m + (zmax + np.log(s))/alpha)*(2.0/n)
```
zmax should 0; if c max repeated, zmax 0.

`smooth_c5_grad`: after f, maybe set small weights to 0? not.
Use `np.cumsum`. For n=1 return f, zero.
Need if `s` zero? exp(z) at max at least e^(0) if z contains 0. zmax maybe positive small; after subtract, max 0. s≥1.

`proj_line_search`: Use `project_box_sum` with target. If grad has nan, return. Armijo `1e-5`? To accept more, use `1e-5`. Since we accept monotone if any. Let's use `c1=1e-5` to allow moderate.
```
def proj_line_search(y, grad, f_y, n, alpha, step):
   target=n*0.5
   g2=float(np.dot(grad,grad))
   if not np.isfinite(g2) or g2 < 1e-20:
       return y.copy(), f_y, step*0.5
   best_h=y.copy(); best_f=f_y
   s=step
   for _ in range(16):
       h1=project_box_sum(y - s*grad, target)
       f1=smooth_objective(h1,n,alpha)
       if f1 < best_f:
          best_h=h1; best_f=f1
       if f1 <= f_y - 1e-5*s*g2:
          return h1, f1, min(s*1.3, 0.5)
       s *= 0.5
       if s < 1e-8: break
   if best_f < f_y - 1e-14:
       return best_h, best_f, max(s,1e-5)
   return y.copy(), f_y, max(s*0.5,1e-6)
```
Cap step 0.5. If f1 much lower but Armijo not due g2 small, still after loop return best. Good.

`gradient_optimize`:
```
def gradient_optimize(h,n,schedule,deadline,step_init=0.1):
  target=n*0.5
  h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
  cur=h.copy()
  for ai,(alpha,iters) in enumerate(schedule):
    if time.time() > deadline: break
    y=cur.copy(); step=step_init; stale=0
    prev=cur.copy()
    for it in range(iters):
      if time.time() > deadline: break
      f_y,g=smooth_c5_grad(y,n,alpha)
      h_new,f_new,step=proj_line_search(y,g,f_y,n,alpha,step)
      if f_new < f_y - 1e-14:
         prev=cur
         cur=h_new
         stale=0
         if it % 15 == 0:
            cex=compute_c5(cur,n)
            if cex < best_c:
               best_c=cex; best_h=cur.copy()
         mom = 0.25 * min(1.0, it/float(it+3))
         if mom>0:
             y=project_box_sum(cur + mom*(cur-prev), target)
         else:
             y=cur.copy()
      else:
         stale +=1
         step=max(step*0.5,1e-7)
         if stale % 50 == 0 and stale>100:
             # reset to cur
             y=cur.copy(); step=step_init; stale=0
             if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
    # after alpha eval exact
    cex=compute_c5(cur,n)
    if cex < best_c:
       best_c=cex; best_h=cur.copy()
    # choose cur for next: if cur exact worse than best by >1e-4, use best
    if cex > best_c + 1e-4:
       cur=best_h.copy()
  cex=compute_c5(cur,n)
  if cex < best_c: best_c=cex; best_h=cur.copy()
  return best_h,best_c
```
Momentum `mom` uses prev old, cur new. In FISTA, y should use current step number, but small. If line search no progress, we don't update y? We keep y stale; after stale, reset. Maybe if no descent, set y=cur to avoid trying same? In else, could set `y=cur.copy()` after some. Let's do:
```
else:
 stale += 1
 step = max(step*0.5,1e-7)
 if stale == 1 or stale%20==0:
    step=step_init
 else: continue
 y=cur.copy() if stale>10 else y
```
Simpler: if no descent, reset y=cur and step step_init after 20 fails, break if too many.
```
else:
 stale+=1
 if stale>=30:
    y=cur.copy(); step=step_init; stale=0
    if fail_alpha? break
 else:
    step=max(step*0.5,1e-7)
```
This prevents stale y.

Maybe gradient plain with no momentum easier. Let's use plain y=cur always to ensure line search from current feasible. Without momentum:
```
y=cur.copy()
...
if accepted: cur=h_new; y=cur.copy()
else: step...; if stale>20: break
```
This is simple, robust. Momentum might complicate. Let's use plain gradient descent with backtracking. It may be slower but safe. We can increase iterations. Use `y=cur.copy()` each iter. Then line search from current.
```
for alpha,iters in schedule:
  cur=best_h? no, start cur from previous.
  step=step_init; stale=0
  for it...
    f_y,g=smooth_c5_grad(cur,n,alpha)
    h_new,...=proj_line_search(cur,g,f_y,...)

    else: stale++; step reduced; if stale>40: break

`gradient_optimize` with plain:
```
def gradient_optimize(h,n,schedule,deadline,step_init=0.1):
  h=project_feasible...
  best...
  cur=h.copy()
  for alpha,iters in schedule:
    if time>deadline: break
    if cur is not best? We can keep cur.
    step=step_init; stale=0
    for it in range(iters):
      if time>deadline: break
      f_y,g=smooth_c5_grad(cur,n,alpha)
      h_new,f_new,step=proj_line_search(cur,g,f_y,n,alpha,step)
      if f_new < f_y - 1e-13:
         cur=h_new; stale=0
         # maybe increase step a bit? line search returned increased already.
         if it % 20 == 0: eval exact
      else:
         stale +=1
         step = max(step*0.5,1e-6)
         if stale >= 50:
             break
    eval exact cur update best
    if compute_c5(cur) > best_c + 2e-4: cur=best_h.copy()
  return best
```
Could get stuck at initial if gradient zero? fine.

Line search Armijo c1=1e-4 maybe with plain. If f_y is smooth current; if no improvement after step reductions, break. Good.

Now `coord_descent` with current c. Let's write robust:
```
def _cd_fval(c_aff,a,diag_idx,has_diag,unaff_max,h0,d):
    vals = c_aff + d*a
    if has_diag:
       vals[diag_idx] = c_aff[diag_idx] + d*(1.0 - h0 - d)
    r=float(np.max(vals))
    if unaff_max > r: r=unaff_max
    return r
```
Note `c_aff` might be a view; `vals` new.

`coord_descent`:
```
def coord_descent(h,n,max_passes,deadline,gold=22,rng=None):
   target=n*0.5
   h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   gr=(sqrt5-1)/2
   for pnum in range(max_passes):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
      order = np.arange(n) if rng is None else rng.permutation(n)
      improved=False; impr_count=0
      for p in order:
         if time.time()>deadline: break
         h0=float(h[p])
         if h0 <= 1e-12 or h0 >= 1.0-1e-12: continue
         m_lo = max(0, n-1-p); m_hi = min(2*n-2, n-1+p)
         if m_hi < m_lo: continue
         aff = c[m_lo:m_hi+1]
         cmax=float(c.max())  # can compute before p? c changed; compute once per p? O(n). We'll compute inside.
         unaff=0.0
         if m_lo>0:
            u=float(np.max(c[:m_lo])); unaff=max(unaff,u)
         if m_hi<2*n-2:
            u=float(np.max(c[m_hi+1:])); unaff=max(unaff,u)
         if unaff >= cmax - 1e-12:
            continue
         # Now p affects all current max? Since unaff < cmax, maximum must be in affected. Good.
         ms=np.arange(m_lo,m_hi+1)
         jf=p+ms-(n-1); jb=p-ms+(n-1)
         vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
         a=np.zeros(ms.size)
         if np.any(vf): a[vf] += (1.0 - h[jf[vf]])
         if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
         dpos=np.where(ms==(n-1))[0]
         has_diag = dpos.size>0
         diag_idx=int(dpos[0]) if has_diag else -1
         c_aff = c[ms]
         d_lo=-h0; d_hi=1.0-h0
         d1=d_hi-gr*(d_hi-d_lo); d2=d_lo+gr*(d_hi-d_lo)
         f1=_cd_fval(c_aff,a,diag_idx,has_diag,unaff,h0,d1)
         f2=_cd_fval(c_aff,a,diag_idx,has_diag,unaff,h0,d2)
         for _ in range(gold):
            if d_hi-d_lo < 1e-14: break
            if f1 < f2:
               d_hi=d2; d2=d1; f2=f1; d1=d_hi-gr*(d_hi-d_lo); f1=_cd_fval(...)
            else:
               d_lo=d1; d1=d2; f1=f2; d2=d_lo+gr*(d_hi-d_lo); f2=_cd_fval(...)
         d_opt=0.5*(d_lo+d_hi)
         f_opt=_cd_fval(...d_opt)
         # optional endpoint compare
         f_end1=_cd_fval(... -h0); if f_end1 < f_opt: d_opt=-h0; f_opt=f_end1
         f_end2=_cd_fval(... 1-h0); if f_end2 < f_opt: d_opt=1-h0; f_opt=f_end2
         if f_opt < cmax - 1e-11:
            delta=d_opt
            if abs(delta) > 1e-14:
               h[p]=h0+delta
               if has_diag:
                  old_diag = c[ms[diag_idx]]
                  c[ms] += delta*a
                  c[ms[diag_idx]] = old_diag + delta*(1.0 - h0 - delta)
               else:
                  c[ms] += delta*a
               improved=True; impr_count += 1
               if impr_count % 50 == 0:
                  h=project_feasible(h,n)
                  c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
                  # after recompute, best maybe unchanged; no break
      if improved:
         h=project_feasible(h,n)
         c_new=compute_c5(h,n)
         if c_new < best_c:
            best_c=c_new; best_h=h.copy()
         else:
            # projection didn't improve; maybe keep best_c
            pass
      else:
         # try threshold tiny values? maybe
         break
   # final exact
   c_final=compute_c5(h,n)
   if c_final < best_c: best_h=h; best_c=c_final
   return best_h, best_c
```

- If recompute c after projection (impr_count %50), update `cmax = float(c.max())`.

Now `make_candidate`. Need pass best_h, best_n, n, rng, mode.
```
def make_candidate(n, mode, best_h, best_n, rng):
  x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
  if mode==0 or best_h is None:
      if best_h is None: return project_feasible(np.full(n,0.5),n)
      xs=np.linspace(0,2,best_n)
      y=np.interp(x,xs,best_h)
      return project_feasible(y,n)
  if mode==1:
      y=np.full(n,0.5)
      for k in range(1,7):
         amp=0.35/(0.8+k)*rng.rand()
         phase=2π*rng.rand()
         y += amp*np.cos(np.pi*k*x + phase)
      return project_feasible(y,n)
  if mode==2:
      # blocks
      num = max(4, min(n//3, 20))
      lengths = rng.randint(3,15,num)
      lengths = (lengths / lengths.sum()*n).astype(int)
      if lengths.sum()!=n: adjust last
      vals=[]
      val = rng.rand()<0.5
      for L in lengths:
         v = 1-val if rng.rand()<0.3 else val? For alternating with occasional same.
         vals.append(v); val = 1-val maybe
      y=np.repeat(vals,lengths)
      # add noise to smooth blocks
      y = y + 0.05*rng.rand(n)
      return project_feasible(y,n)
  if mode==3:
      freq = int(rng.randint(1,7))
      phase=2π*rng.rand(); beta=rng.uniform(1,6)
      y=0.5+0.5*np.tanh(beta*np.sin(π*freq*x+phase))
      return project_feasible(y,n)
  if mode==4:
      # symmetric around n/2? y[i] + y[n-1-i]=1
      y=np.full(n,0.5)
      half=n//2
      y[:half]=rng.rand(half)
      y[n-half:]=1-y[:half]
      if n%2: y[half]=0.5
      return project_feasible(y,n)
  if mode==5 and best_h is not None:
      y=np.interp(x,np.linspace(0,2,best_n),best_h) + 0.05*rng.normal(0,1,n)
      return project_feasible(y,n)
  if mode==6 and best_h is not None:
      y=np.interp(...)
      # push extremes: y = np.clip(0.5 + 1.8*(y-0.5),0,1)? maybe gain
      y = np.clip(0.5 + 2.0*(y-0.5),0.0,1.0)
      return project_feasible(y,n)
  default mode0
```

`optimize_candidate`:
```
def optimize_candidate(h,n,deadline,cd_passes=20,gold=20,use_grad=True,rng=None):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if use_grad and time.time()<deadline:
        if n <= 150: sched=[(50,500),(500,500),(5000,300)]
        elif n <= 300: sched=[(50,350),(500,350),(5000,200)]
        elif n <= 600: sched=[(50,250),(500,250),(5000,150)]
        else: sched=[(50,150),(500,150),(5000,100)]
        h1,c1=gradient_optimize(h,n,sched,deadline-10,step_init=0.1)
        if c1 < c or True: h,c=h1,c1
    if time.time()<deadline-5:
        h2,c2=coord_descent(h,n,cd_passes,deadline-2,gold=gold,rng=rng)
        if c2 < c: h,c=h2,c2
    return h,c
```

Let's plan code for CD with loop over L:
```
def coord_descent(h,n,max_passes,deadline,gold=20,rng=None):
    h=project_feasible...
    best...
    pos=detect_pos(n)
    center=n-1
    gr=0.618...
    for pass:
       if time>deadline: break
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       cmax=float(c.max())
       order = np.arange(n) if rng is None else rng.permutation(n)
       changed=False; impr=0
       for p in order:
           if time>deadline: break
           h0=float(h[p])
           if h0<=1e-12 or h0>=1-1e-12: continue
           Lmax = max(p, center-p)
           m_lo = center - Lmax
           m_hi = center + Lmax
           # unaff
           unaff=0.0
           if m_lo>0: u=float(np.max(c[:m_lo])); unaff=unaff if unaff>u else u
           if m_hi<2*center: u=float(np.max(c[m_hi+1:])); unaff=unaff if unaff>u else u
           if unaff >= cmax - 1e-11: continue
           half = Lmax
           a = np.zeros(2*half+1)
           # loop L
           # local variables for speed
           hh=h
           n1 = center
           for L in range(1, half+1):
               coeffA=0.0; coeffB=0.0
               if p <= n1 - L:
                   coeffA += 1.0 - hh[p+L]
               if p >= L:
                   coeffA -= hh[p-L]
               if p >= L:
                   coeffB += 1.0 - hh[p-L]
               if p <= n1 - L:
                   coeffB -= hh[p+L]
               if pos:
                   a[half + L] = coeffA
                   a[half - L] = coeffB
               else:
                   a[half + L] = coeffB
                   a[half - L] = coeffA
           # if half=0? n=1 no, but for n>1 half>=? p center? n=1 only. For n=2 center=1, p=0 half=1, p=1 half=1.
           c_aff = c[m_lo:m_hi+1]
           diag = half
           # fcurr = cmax (since unaff < cmax and affected max cmax)
           d_lo=-h0; d_hi=1.0-h0
           d1=d_hi-gr*(d_hi-d_lo); d2=d_lo+gr*(d_hi-d_lo)
           f1=_cd_fval(c_aff,a,diag,True,unaff,h0,d1)
           f2=_cd_fval(...)
           for _ in range(gold): ...
           d_opt=...
           f_opt=...
           # endpoints
           fe=f_end_lo; if fe<f_opt...
           if f_opt < cmax - 1e-12:
              delta=d_opt
              if abs(delta)>1e-14:
                 h[p]=h0+delta
                 old_diag=float(c[center])  # c[center] current
                 c[m_lo:m_hi+1] += delta*a? Using slice assignment.
                 c[center] = old_diag + delta*(1.0 - h0 - delta)
                 changed=True; impr+=1; cmax=f_opt
                 if impr % 50 ==0:
                    h=project_feasible(h,n)
                    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
                    cmax=float(c.max())
       if changed:
           h=project_feasible(h,n)
           cnew=compute_c5(h,n)
           if cnew<best_c: update
       else: break
    final...
```
Slice `c[m_lo:m_hi+1] += delta*a` works with basic slice, in-place okay. Because a same length. Good. `c[center]` in slice, but we override after. Note `old_diag` should be before adding. Good.

Code compact:
```
def make_candidate(n, mode, best_h, best_n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0,2,n)
    if mode == 0 or best_h is None:
       if best_h is None: return project_feasible(np.full(n,0.5),n)
       return project_feasible(np.interp(x,np.linspace(0,2,best_n),best_h),n)
    base = np.interp(x,np.linspace(0,2,best_n),best_h) if best_h is not None else np.full(n,0.5)
    if mode == 1:
       return project_feasible(base + 0.05*rng.randn(n),n)
    if mode == 2:
       y=np.full(n,0.5)
       for k in range(1,8):
           y += 0.4/(0.5+k)*rng.rand()*np.cos(np.pi*k*x + 2*np.pi*rng.rand())
       return project_feasible(y,n)
    if mode == 3:
       f=int(rng.randint(1,7)); ph=2*np.pi*rng.rand(); b=rng.uniform(1,6)
       return project_feasible(0.5+0.5*np.tanh(b*np.sin(np.pi*f*x+ph)),n)
    # mode 4 blocks
    nb=max(3,min(20,n//8))
    bs=np.sort(rng.choice(np.arange(1,n), nb-1, replace=False))
    edges=np.concatenate(([0],bs,[n]))
    lengths=np.diff(edges)
    start=rng.rand()<0.5
    y=np.empty(n); pos=0
    for i,L in enumerate(lengths):
       val = (start != (i%2==0))  # bool
       y[pos:pos+L] = 1.0 if val else 0.0
       pos += L
    return project_feasible(y + 0.04*rng.rand(n), n)
```
Start mode maybe. Good.

`mode 0` if best_h None returns constant; but constant C=0.5, CD may not improve? Actually coordinate descent on constant cmax? h=0.5 all, c0 max? c0=0.5n? c_lags all? Swapping? Active skip: unaff? For coordinate p, Lmax; unaff maybe cmax? If all equal, p affects all max? unaff maybe cmax if outside; skip. It may get stuck. So random starts better. Initial from parent good.

Detailed `coord_descent`:
```
def coord_descent(h, n, max_passes, deadline, gold=20, rng=None):
    target = 0.5*n
    h = project_feasible(h,n).astype(float, copy=True)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    center=n-1
    pos=detect_pos(n)
    gr=0.6180339887498949
    no_progress=0
    for pass_i in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        order = np.arange(n) if rng is None else rng.permutation(n)
        changed=False
        impr=0
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            h0 = float(h[p])
            if h0 <= 1e-12 or h0 >= 1.0-1e-12: continue
            Lmax = p if p > center-p else center-p
            if Lmax == 0:
                Lmax = 0
            m_lo = center - Lmax
            m_hi = center + Lmax
            unaff = 0.0
            if m_lo > 0:
                unaff = float(np.max(c[:m_lo]))
            if m_hi < 2*center:
                u = float(np.max(c[m_hi+1:]))
                if u > unaff: unaff = u
            if unaff >= cmax - 1e-11:
                continue
            vec = np.empty(2*Lmax+1)
            vec.fill(0.0)
            # loop
            for L in range(1, Lmax+1):
                ca = 0.0; cb=0.0
                if p <= center - L:
                    ca += 1.0 - h[p+L]
                if p >= L:
                    ca -= h[p-L]
                    cb += 1.0 - h[p-L]
                if p <= center - L:
                    cb -= h[p+L]
                if pos:
                    vec[Lmax+L] = ca
                    vec[Lmax-L] = cb
                else:
                    vec[Lmax+L] = cb
                    vec[Lmax-L] = ca
            c_aff = c[m_lo:m_hi+1]
            diag = Lmax
            d_lo = -h0; d_hi = 1.0-h0
            d1 = d_hi - gr*(d_hi-d_lo)
            d2 = d_lo + gr*(d_hi-d_lo)
            f1 = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d1)
            f2 = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d2)
            for _ in range(gold):
                if d_hi - d_lo < 1e-15: break
                if f1 < f2:
                    d_hi = d2; d2 = d1; f2 = f1
                    d1 = d_hi - gr*(d_hi - d_lo)
                    f1 = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d1)
                else:
                    d_lo = d1; d1 = d2; f1 = f2
                    d2 = d_lo + gr*(d_hi - d_lo)
                    f2 = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d2)
            d_opt = 0.5*(d_lo+d_hi)
            f_opt = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d_opt)
            fe = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d_lo)
            if fe < f_opt: d_opt=d_lo; f_opt=fe
            fe = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d_hi)
            if fe < f_opt: d_opt=d_hi; f_opt=fe
            if f_opt < cmax - 1e-11:
                delta = d_opt
                if abs(delta) > 1e-14:
                    h[p] = h0 + delta
                    old_diag = float(c[center])
                    c[m_lo:m_hi+1] += delta*vec
                    c[center] = old_diag + delta*(1.0 - h0 - delta)
                    cmax = f_opt
                    changed = True
                    impr += 1
                    if (impr & 0x1f) == 0:  # every 32
                        h = project_feasible(h,n)
                        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
                        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if changed:
            h = project_feasible(h,n)
            cnew = compute_c5(h,n)
            if cnew < best_c:
                best_c = cnew; best_h = h.copy(); no_progress = 0
            else:
                no_progress += 1
                if no_progress >= 3:
                    break
        else:
            break
    cfinal = compute_c5(h,n)
    if cfinal < best_c:
        best_c = cfinal; best_h = h
    return best_h, best_c
```
Check `(impr & 0x1f)==0` when impr=32,64. But impr starts 0, after increment to 32 triggers. Good. If no improvement, not.

If `Lmax=0` (n=1), vec length1, loop none, unaff maybe 0, cmax? Golden d interval; no active? n=1 trivial. But run n>1. Fine.

But if both conditions true, ca = 1-h[p+L] - h[p-L]; cb = 1-h[p-L] - h[p+L]. Good.

For p edge, Lmax high; loop many. Good.

Let's optimize `project_box_sum` by using local variables and 36 iterations. Code:
```
def project_box_sum(y,target):
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    ymin=float(np.min(y)); ymax=float(np.max(y))
    if ymin >= -1e-12 and ymax <= 1.0+1e-12:
        s=float(np.sum(y))
        if abs(s-target) < 1e-8:
            h=np.clip(y,0,1); ... adjust; return h
    lo=-ymax; hi=1.0-ymin
    for _ in range(45):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        s=float(np.sum(np.clip(y+mid,0.0,1.0)))
        if s < target: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(y+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       h=h.copy()
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12)
       if mask.any(): h[mask]+=diff/float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
       else:
          if diff>0: idx=np.where(h<1.0)[0]
          else: idx=np.where(h>0.0)[0]
          if idx.size: h[idx]+=diff/float(idx.size)
       np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return h
```
In shortcut, if y feasible and sum close, need adjust diff. Use helper? Just not shortcut to keep simple. Maybe no shortcut for correctness. But projection frequent; bisection okay. We can skip shortcut.

Need if hi < lo? y all >1? lo=-ymax negative large, hi=1-ymin negative maybe greater? Example y=[2,2], ymin=ymax=2, lo=-2, hi=-1, ok. y all negative -2: lo=2, hi=3, ok.

Now `make_candidate` uses `project_feasible`. For mode0 if best_h None and n maybe, returns 0.5 constant. For mode3 with best None base not used. Good.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+max(10,budget_s)-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h_init=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float)
    except Exception:
       h_init=None
    if h_init is not None and len(h_init)>=2:
       pn=int(len(h_init)); ph=h_init.copy()
    else:
       pn=200; ph=make_candidate(pn,3,None,0,rng)
    # If pn>900 downsample to 700? Since prefer <1k; if >900, set pn=700 and ph interp.
    if pn > 900:
       new_n=700; ph=np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,new_n), np.linspace(0,2,pn), ph); pn=new_n
    best_h=project_feasible(ph,pn); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,pn); best_n=pn
    # baseline CD
    h2,c2=coord_descent(best_h,best_n,80, min(deadline-300, t0+0.45*budget_s), 24, rng)
    if c2<best_c: update
    # If still above target, a few perturbations same n
    if best_c > 0.38085 and time.time()<deadline-250:
       for r in range(4):
          if time.time()>deadline-220: break
          modes = [1,4,3]
          h0=make_candidate(best_n, modes[r%3], best_h,best_n,rng)
          lim=min(deadline-200, time.time()+90)
          h2,c2=coord_descent(h0,best_n,40,lim,22,rng)
          update
    # multi-n
    n_set = [100,150,200,250,300,350,400,450,500,550,600]
    if best_n<=900: n_set.append(best_n)
    n_list=sorted(set([n for n in n_set if 50<=n<=850 and n!=best_n])) # maybe include best? exploration best separate
    for nt in n_list:
       if time.time()>deadline-100: break
       modes = [0,1,3,4] if nt<=250 else [0,1,4]
       for md in modes:
          if time.time()>deadline-90: break
          h0=make_candidate(nt,md,best_h,best_n,rng)
          if compute_c5(project_feasible(h0,nt),nt) >= best_c: # could skip if already worse? But CD might improve; skip maybe no.
              # don't skip; maybe if much worse > best_c+0.05 skip
              pass
          passes = 120 if nt<=120 else 80 if nt<=220 else 50 if nt<=350 else 30
          alloc = 25 if nt<=150 else 35 if nt<=300 else 45 if nt<=500 else 60
          lim = min(deadline-80, time.time()+alloc)
          h2,c2=coord_descent(h0,nt,passes,lim,18 if nt>300 else 20,rng)
          if c2<best_c: update
    # final CD on best
    if time.time()<deadline-8:
       h2,c2=coord_descent(best_h,best_n,40,deadline-2,24,rng)
       if c2<best_c: update
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=float(compute_c5(best_h,best_n))
    return best_h,best_c,int(best_n)

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

POS_CACHE = {}


def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full"))) * dx


def project_box_sum(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    lo = -float(np.max(y))
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(y))
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(y + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        h = h.copy()
        mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if mask.any():
            h[mask] += diff / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        else:
            if diff > 0:
                idx = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
            else:
                idx = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
            if idx.size:
                h[idx] += diff / float(idx.size)
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def project_feasible(h, n):
    return project_box_sum(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.5 * n)


def detect_pos(n):
    if n in POS_CACHE:
        return POS_CACHE[n]
    if n < 2:
        POS_CACHE[n] = True
        return True
    h = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a1 = float(np.dot(h[:-1], 1.0 - h[1:]))
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    center = n - 1
    pos = abs(c[center + 1] - a1) <= abs(c[center - 1] - a1)
    POS_CACHE[n] = pos
    return pos


def _cd_fval(c_aff, a, diag, has_diag, unaff_max, h0, d):
    vals = c_aff + d * a
    if has_diag:
        vals[diag] = c_aff[diag] + d * (1.0 - h0 - d)
    r = float(np.max(vals))
    if unaff_max > r:
        r = unaff_max
    return r


def coord_descent(h, n, max_passes, deadline, gold=20, rng=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    center = n - 1
    pos = detect_pos(n)
    gr = 0.6180339887498949
    no_prog = 0

    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        order = np.arange(n) if rng is None else rng.permutation(n)
        changed = False
        impr = 0

        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h0 = float(h[p])
            if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-13:
                continue

            lmax = p if p > center - p else center - p
            lo = center - lmax
            hi = center + lmax

            unaff = 0.0
            if lo > 0:
                unaff = float(np.max(c[:lo]))
            if hi < 2 * center:
                u = float(np.max(c[hi + 1:]))
                if u > unaff:
                    unaff = u
            if unaff >= cmax - 1e-11:
                continue

            vec = np.zeros(2 * lmax + 1, dtype=float)
            for l in range(1, lmax + 1):
                ca = 0.0
                cb = 0.0
                if p <= center - l:
                    ca += 1.0 - h[p + l]
                    cb -= h[p + l]
                if p >= l:
                    ca -= h[p - l]
                    cb += 1.0 - h[p - l]
                if pos:
                    vec[lmax + l] = ca
                    vec[lmax - l] = cb
                else:
                    vec[lmax + l] = cb
                    vec[lmax - l] = ca

            c_aff = c[lo:hi + 1]
            diag = lmax
            d_lo = -h0
            d_hi = 1.0 - h0
            d1 = d_hi - gr * (d_hi - d_lo)
            d2 = d_lo + gr * (d_hi - d_lo)
            f1 = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d1)
            f2 = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, diag == 0 or True, unaff, h0, d2)

            for _ in range(gold):
                if d_hi - d_lo < 1e-15:
                    break
                if f1 < f2:
                    d_hi, d2, f2 = d2, d1, f1
                    d1 = d_hi - gr * (d_hi - d_lo)
                    f1 = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d1)
                else:
                    d_lo, d1, f1 = d1, d2, f2
                    d2 = d_lo + gr * (d_hi - d_lo)
                    f2 = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d2)

            d_opt = 0.5 * (d_lo + d_hi)
            f_opt = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d_opt)
            fe = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d_lo)
            if fe < f_opt:
                d_opt, f_opt = d_lo, fe
            fe = _cd_fval(c_aff, vec, diag, True, unaff, h0, d_hi)
            if fe < f_opt:
                d_opt, f_opt = d_hi, fe

            if f_opt < cmax - 1e-11:
                d = d_opt
                if abs(d) > 1e-14:
                    h[p] = h0 + d
                    old_diag = float(c[center])
                    c[lo:hi + 1] += d * vec
                    c[center] = old_diag + d * (1.0 - h0 - d)
                    cmax = f_opt
                    changed = True
                    impr += 1
                    if (impr & 0x1f) == 0:
                        h = project_feasible(h, n)
                        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                        cmax = float(np.max(c))

        if changed:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            cnew = compute_c5(h, n)
            if cnew < best_c:
                best_c = cnew
                best_h = h.copy()
                no_prog = 0
            else:
                no_prog += 1
                if no_prog >= 3:
                    break
        else:
            break

    cf = compute_c5(h, n)
    if cf < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = h.copy(), cf
    return best_h, best_c


def make_candidate(n, mode, ref_h, ref_n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    if ref_h is None or len(ref_h) == 0:
        ref_h = np.full(n, 0.5)
        ref_n = n

    if mode == 0 or ref_h is None:
        xs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, ref_n)
        return project_feasible(np.interp(x, xs, ref_h), n)

    base = None
    if ref_h is not None and len(ref_h) > 0:
        base = np.interp(x, np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, ref_n), ref_h)

    if mode == 1:
        if base is None:
            base = np.full(n, 0.5)
        return project_feasible(base + 0.05 * rng.randn(n), n)

    if mode == 2:
        y = np.full(n, 0.5)
        for k in range(1, 8):
            y += (0.45 / (0.5 + k)) * rng.rand() * np.cos(math.pi * k * x + 2.0 * math.pi * rng.rand())
        return project_feasible(y, n)

    if mode == 3:
        f = int(rng.randint(1, 7))
        ph = 2.0 * math.pi * rng.rand()
        b = rng.uniform(1.0, 6.0)
        return project_feasible(0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(b * np.sin(math.pi * f * x + ph)), n)

    nb = max(3, min(20, n // 8))
    edges = np.sort(rng.choice(np.arange(1, n), nb - 1, replace=False))
    edges = np.concatenate(([0], edges, [n]))
    lengths = np.diff(edges)
    start = rng.rand() < 0.5
    y = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    pos = 0
    for i, el in enumerate(lengths):
        v = (start != (i % 2 == 0))
        y[pos:pos + el] = 1.0 if v else 0.0
        pos += int(el)
    return project_feasible(y + 0.04 * rng.rand(n), n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(20, int(budget_s)) - 5
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h0 = None

    if h0 is None or len(h0) < 2:
        n0 = 200
        h0 = make_candidate(n0, 3, None, 0, rng)
    else:
        n0 = int(len(h0))

    if n0 > 900:
        h0 = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, 700), np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0), h0)
        n0 = 700

    best_h = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)
    best_n = n0
    ref_h = best_h.copy()
    ref_n = n0

    h1, c1 = coord_descent(best_h, best_n, 120, min(deadline - 320, t0 + 0.35 * budget_s), 24, rng)
    if c1 < best_c:
        best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, best_n
        ref_h, ref_n = best_h.copy(), best_n

    if best_c > 0.38084 and time.time() < deadline - 260:
        for r in range(5):
            if time.time() > deadline - 230:
                break
            md = [1, 4, 3, 1, 4][r % 5]
            h_s = make_candidate(best_n, md, best_h, best_n, rng)
            lim = min(deadline - 210, time.time() + 95)
            h2, c2 = coord_descent(h_s, best_n, 45, lim, 22, rng)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, best_n
                ref_h, ref_n = best_h.copy(), best_n
        ref_h, ref_n = best_h.copy(), best_n

    n_list = [100, 140, 180, 220, 260, 300, 340, 400, 460, 520, 580]
    if best_n <= 850:
        n_list.append(best_n)
    n_list = sorted(set([m for m in n_list if 60 <= m <= 850]))

    for nt in n_list:
        if time.time() > deadline - 110:
            break
        modes = [0, 1, 3, 4] if nt <= 260 else [0, 1, 4]
        for md in modes:
            if time.time() > deadline - 100:
                break
            if md == 0:
                hs = make_candidate(nt, md, ref_h, ref_n, rng)
            else:
                hs = make_candidate(nt, md, best_h, best_n, rng)
            passes = 140 if nt <= 140 else 90 if nt <= 240 else 55 if nt <= 380 else 30
            alloc = 26 if nt <= 170 else 36 if nt <= 320 else 48 if nt <= 520 else 60
            lim = min(deadline - 90, time.time() + alloc)
            gold = 20 if nt <= 320 else 18
            h2, c2 = coord_descent(hs, nt, passes, lim, gold, rng)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, nt
                ref_h, ref_n = best_h.copy(), best_n

    if time.time() < deadline - 8:
        h2, c2 = coord_descent(best_h, best_n, 50, deadline - 2, 24, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, best_n

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = float(compute_c5(best_h, best_n))
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_n)
```
